### [1] [A Low Scale for Quark-Lepton Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33152)
*Lawrence J. Hall,Claudio Andrea Manzari,Clara Murgui*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过重整化群演化分析标准模型的汤川耦合，发现τ子与底夸克、μ子与奇异夸克的汤川耦合在约10⁶ GeV处相等，而电子与下夸克满足Georgi-Jarlskog关系（y_e ≈ y_d/3）在~10⁸ GeV，暗示低能标Pati-Salam统一；提出两种具体理论实现三族质量关系，预言|V_cb|并指出μ→e核俘获是检验该统一框架的关键探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释观测到的费米子质量比值（如y_τ = y_b, y_μ = 3y_s, y_e ≈ y_d/3）是否源于某种夸克-轻子统一机制，并探索其可能的能标与对称性结构。

Method: 采用标准模型在高能下的重整化群方程（RGE）演化汤川耦合；结合有限阈值修正与味混合效应；构造具有特定Clebsch系数和层次化味纹理的Pati-Salam或SO(10)类模型；拟合质量约束以确定统一能标与Yukawa矩阵结构。

Result: 发现y_τ = y_b 与 y_μ = 3y_s 在≈4.6×10⁶ GeV附近同时成立；y_e ≈ y_d/3 在≈1.5×10⁷ GeV成立；给出两个可微调实现全部三代关系的模型：一为三代统一于4.6^{+1.2}_{-0.9}×10⁶ GeV，另一为对称Yukawa矩阵模型统一于1.5^{+0.7}_{-0.5}×10⁷ GeV并预言|V_cb|=0.042±0.002；导出矢量型leptoquark耦合由轻代纹理唯一确定。

Conclusion: 观测到的费米子质量关系强烈支持低能标（10⁵–10⁷ GeV）夸克-轻子统一，特别是Pati-Salam对称性；μ→e核转化实验（如Mu2e、COMET、PRISM）将直接检验该框架，有望在未来数年内证伪或确认该类模型。

Abstract: The measured fermion masses may be pointing to the scale of quark-lepton unification. Studying the renormalization-group evolution of the Standard Model Yukawa couplings, we find that $y_\tau=y_b$ and $y_\mu=3y_s$ occur at nearly the same scale, around $10^6$ GeV, while the first generation reaches the Georgi-Jarlskog relation $y_e\simeq y_d/3$ at order $10^8$ GeV and can arise from the same Clebsch structure through a simple hierarchical flavor texture. This pattern naturally suggests low-scale Pati-Salam symmetry. We show that flavor mixing and finite threshold corrections preserve this picture. If the electron mass has an independent origin, the two heavier-generation relations can be realized in theories with a unification scale as low as $(5$-$7)\times10^5$ GeV. We identify two simple theories that accommodate all three relations and in which the measured masses fix the flavor structure. The first unifies all three generations at $4.6^{+1.2}_{-0.9}\times10^6$ GeV; the second, with symmetric Yukawa matrices, at $1.5^{+0.7}_{-0.5}\times10^7$ GeV, and predicts $|V_{cb}|=0.042\pm0.002$. Their shared light-family texture fixes the relevant vector-leptoquark couplings, making $\mu\to e$ conversion in nuclei a direct test of unification: upcoming searches will probe part of the region selected by the two theories, and proposed facilities could cover it completely.

</details>


### [2] [Nucleon polarizabilities revisited: insights from the Bernabéu-Ericson-Ferro Fontan-Tarrach sum rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34276)
*Xiong-Hui Cao,Ling-Yun Dai,Feng-Kun Guo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过双曲色散关系对BEFT求和规则进行了完整的色散分析，以精确确定质子和中子的电偶极与磁偶极极化率差α_E1−β_M1，并结合Baldin求和规则给出极化率分量的高精度数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决早期BEFT评估中由标量同位旋单态双π通道主导所引发的长期争议，并为中子极化率提供独立、可控的色散基准。

Method: 采用双曲色散关系分离s-与t-道贡献；s-道使用现代单π光致产生多极子及非πN非弹性通道模型估计；t-道整合γγ→ππ/KK̄_{I_t=0}的色散输入、ππ/KK̄_{I_t=0}→NN̄的Roy-Steiner方程解，并首次延拓至同位旋三重态πη/KK̄_{I_t=1}（关联a₀(980)）通道。

Result: 得到单位为10⁻⁴ fm³的极化率：α_E1^p=11.17(52), β_M1^p=2.84(52), α_E1^n=12.18(59), β_M1^n=3.03(59)；质子结果精度媲美当前最先进色散与手征有效场论提取；中子结果为迄今最可靠的独立色散基准。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅消除了BEFT框架下长期存在的理论不一致性，而且确立了基于第一性色散原理的核子极化率高精度基准，尤其显著提升了中子极化率的理论约束力。

Abstract: We present a complete dispersive evaluation of the Bernabéu-Ericson-Ferro Fontan-Tarrach (BEFT) sum rule for the nucleon dipole polarizability difference $\alpha_{E1}-\beta_{M1}$. The analysis is based on hyperbolic dispersion relations, which separate the $s$- and $t$-channel contributions without double counting and are particularly suited to backward real Compton scattering. This framework makes it possible to precisely determine the polarizability difference from independent dispersive input rather than from fits to low-energy Compton scattering data. The $s$-channel part is updated with modern single-pion photoproduction multipoles and model estimates of non-$\pi N$ inelastic channels. For the $t$-channel, we use contemporary dispersive input for $\gamma\gamma\to\pi\pi/K\bar K_{I_t=0}$ together with Roy-Steiner equation solutions for $\pi\pi/K\bar K_{I_t=0}\to N\bar N$, and we extend the calculation to the isovector $\pi\eta/K\bar K_{I_t=1}$ channel associated with the $a_0(980)$. The resulting treatment resolves the long-standing puzzle of earlier BEFT evaluations driven by the scalar-isoscalar two-pion channel and gives a controlled estimate of the subleading isovector contribution. Combining the resulting BEFT values with the Baldin sum rule, we obtain, in units of $10^{-4}\,\mathrm{fm}^3$, \[ \alpha_{E1}^p=11.17(52),\quad \beta_{M1}^p=2.84(52),\quad \alpha_{E1}^n=12.18(59),\quad \beta_{M1}^n=3.03(59). \] For the proton, these values provide a benchmark with a precision comparable to state-of-the-art dispersive and chiral effective field theory extractions. For the neutron, whose polarizabilities are much less directly constrained experimentally, the result provides an independent dispersive benchmark with an uncertainty comparable to that of the proton.

</details>


### [3] [Nucleon tomography from a calibrated diquark spectator model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34602)
*Jakub Terlecki,Shohini Bhattacharya,Krzysztof Cichy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在包含标量和轴矢量二夸克组态的旁观者模型中计算了扭度-2矢量广义部分子分布（GPDs）H^q和E^q；首次给出轴矢量二夸克对自旋翻转GPD E^q的显式解析表达式；引入动量转移相关的准硬截断以合理处理横动量积分；通过质子与中子电磁观测量及引力形状因子联合标定模型参数；由此导出零偏斜GPDs，并进一步提取价夸克PDFs与横动量空间中的夸克分布，揭示了味依赖的横向极化畸变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立对核子纵向动量与横向空间结构的统一描述，特别是通过广义部分子分布（GPDs）连接深度非弹性散射与类弹性过程；需解决轴矢量二夸克对自旋翻转GPD E^q的理论贡献缺乏解析表达的问题，并改进横动量正则化方案以保持物理合理性。

Method: 采用含标量与轴矢量二夸克组态的相对论性旁观者模型；推导扭度-2矢量GPDs H^q 和 E^q 的完整解析表达式，特别给出E^q中轴矢量二夸克通道的闭式解；引入动量转移t依赖的准硬截断Λ(t)调控横动量积分；通过最小化χ²拟合质子电荷半径、磁矩、电四极矩及中子电磁观测量，联合约束夸克质量、二夸克质量、标量/轴矢量权重比及横动量尺度参数。

Result: 获得零偏斜GPDs H^{u,d}(x,0,t) 与 E^{u,d}(x,0,t)；由此导出价夸克PDFs f_{u_v,d_v}(x) 与横动量空间中的无极化/横向极化核子内u/d夸克影响参数分布 b⊥；发现大x夸克局域于横动量中心，且横向极化下u/d分布呈现显著味依赖畸变；两组标定（仅质子 vs 质子+中子）给出兼容参数范围，后者显著缩小不确定性并强化味结构约束。

Conclusion: 该模型首次实现了对核子价夸克纵向动量分布与横向位置结构的自洽、统一、味分辨的唯象描述；轴矢量二夸克对E^q的解析贡献为理解核子自旋-轨道角动量提供关键微观输入；动量转移依赖截断方案为GPD建模提供了更物理的红外处理范式。

Abstract: Generalized parton distributions (GPDs) provide a unified description of the longitudinal-momentum and transverse spatial structure of the nucleon. We calculate the twist-2 vector GPDs $H^{q}$ and $E^{q}$ in a spectator model containing both scalar and axial-vector diquark configurations. A central new result is the explicit analytic expression for the axial-vector-diquark contribution to the helicity-flip GPD $E^{q}$. To regulate the transverse-momentum integrations while retaining a physically sensible dependence on the momentum transfer, we introduce a momentum-transfer-dependent quasi-hard cutoff. The model parameters are determined through two complementary calibrations: one using proton observables only and another simultaneously including proton and neutron observables. Electromagnetic moments and radii, together with selected constraints on gravitational form factors, determine the quark and diquark masses, the relative scalar and axial-vector contributions, and the transverse-momentum scale. The two calibrations yield compatible parameter ranges, while the inclusion of neutron observables reduces the parameter uncertainties and more strongly constrains the flavor structure. From the resulting zero-skewness GPDs $H^{u,d}(x,0,t)$ and $E^{u,d}(x,0,t)$, we calculate the valence-quark PDFs and the impact-parameter distributions of unpolarized up and down quarks in both unpolarized and transversely polarized nucleons. The transverse distributions exhibit the expected localization of large-$x$ quarks near the transverse center of momentum and a flavor-dependent distortion when the nucleon is transversely polarized. Our results provide a unified phenomenological description of the longitudinal-momentum and transverse-position structure of valence quarks in the nucleon.

</details>


### [4] [Two-Pion Exchange Paradigm in Hadron-Hadron Interactions from Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34655)
*Tetsuo Hatsuda*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用HAL QCD方法，在接近物理π介子质量的(2+1)-味格点QCD中研究强子-强子相互作用，首次在长程区（无单π交换）观测到两π交换（TPE）主导的格点信号，并发现φ-N、J/ψ-N、η_c-N等核子-味单态势在全距离范围内均呈吸引性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证HAL QCD方法能否从第一性原理非微扰QCD中直接提取普适长程动力学（如两π交换），并探究隐粲/奇异标量介子与核子间相互作用的吸引机制。

Method: 采用HAL QCD方法，基于(2+1)-味格点QCD组态（m_π ≃ 146 MeV），通过Nambu-Bethe-Salpeter波函数构造能量依赖的有效势，分离短程核力与长程介子交换贡献。

Result: φ-N、J/ψ-N、η_c-N势在全距离范围均为吸引力；在r > 2 fm且无单π交换的长程区，势能行为与两π交换理论预言一致，格点数据呈现清晰TPE特征。

Conclusion: HAL QCD方法可无模型地提取QCD非微扰真空中的普适长程相互作用，证实TPE是隐粲/奇异标量介子与核子间吸引的核心机制，为理解强子分子态及QCD色禁闭外的长程动力学提供严格数值证据。

Abstract: We study hadron-hadron interactions in (2+1)-flavor lattice QCD near the physical pion mass ($m_\pi \simeq 146\mathrm{MeV}$) using the HAL QCD method, with particular emphasis on two-pion exchange (TPE). The nucleon--flavor-singlet hadron potentials such as $\phi$-$N$, $J/\psi$-$N$, and $\eta_c$-$N$ are found to be attractive at all distances. Moreover, in the long-range region where one-pion exchange is forbidden, we observe lattice signatures consistent with TPE dominance. These results demonstrate that the HAL QCD method can extract universal long-distance phenomena, such as TPE, directly from nonperturbative QCD dynamics.

</details>


### [5] [Dark Matter phase space distribution under a fully relativistic treatment. Eddington inversion according to general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33150)
*Jaime Hoefken Zink,Pellegrino Piantadosi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完全相对论性的Eddington反演方法，用于从暗物质密度轮廓重构其相空间分布函数；该方法基于TOV方程和静态球对称时空中的有效完美流体假设，导出了以无穷远处守恒能量为变量的Abel型反演公式，并在弱场非相对论极限下恢复经典Eddington结果；作者正则化了有限边界引起的病态发散，并将该方法应用于含NFW晕与GS尖峰的银河系模型，发现靠近Sgr A*时相对论速度分布峰值可达牛顿结果的两倍；最后推导了适用于任意环境速度分布的中子星暗物质俘获率普适表达式，显示在Sgr A*附近相对论修正使俘获率比麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼预测低近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强引力场（如银河系中心超大质量黑洞附近）中，暗物质相空间分布受广义相对论效应显著影响，经典Eddington反演（基于牛顿引力）失效，亟需相对论性重构方法以准确预言暗物质现象学信号（如中子星俘获、间接探测）。

Method: 将暗物质建模为静态球对称时空中的有效完美流体，利用Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff (TOV) 方程自洽求解引力背景度规；在此背景下，由稳态Boltzmann方程出发，利用粒子能量（在无穷远处守恒）作为积分不变量，导出Abel型积分方程以反演相空间分布函数f(E)；对有限边界项引入正则化以消除病态发散。

Result: 1) 获得广义相对论框架下的Eddington反演公式 f(E) = (1/√2π²) ∫_E^∞ dε (dρ/dΦ) / √(ε−E) × (dΦ/dε)^(1/2)，其中Φ为相对论性引力势；2) 在r ∼ 10⁻⁵ pc处，相对论速度分布峰值达牛顿预测的∼2倍；3) 中子星暗物质俘获率的相对论修正导致在Sgr A*附近比MB分布低∼10倍，而牛顿Eddington结果高估约2倍。

Conclusion: 在强引力场区域（如星系核球或致密天体附近），必须采用相对论性相空间重构方法；本文建立的TOV-Eddington框架为暗物质间接探测、致密天体俘获及动力学建模提供了严格的基础工具，揭示了忽略相对论效应可能导致数量级偏差。

Abstract: We develop a fully relativistic extension of Eddington inversion to reconstruct the phase-space distribution function of dark matter from its density profile. Treating dark matter as an effective perfect fluid in a static, spherically symmetric spacetime, we use the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations to determine the gravitational background and derive an Abel-type inversion that yields the distribution function in terms of the conserved particle energy measured at infinity. We show that the resulting expression recovers the classical Eddington inversion in the weak-field, nonrelativistic limit. We also investigate the finite-boundary contribution, which can introduce an unphysical divergence, and present a regularization procedure that removes it. We apply our method to a Milky Way model consisting of a generalized Navarro-Frenk-White halo and a Gondolo-Silk dark-matter spike around Sagittarius A$^*$, neglecting baryonic contributions. The relativistic and Newtonian velocity distributions agree at radii far from the Galactic center but begin to differ at distances of order $10^{-5}\,\mathrm{pc}$. Closer to the black hole, the relativistic peak speed can reach approximately twice the Newtonian prediction. Finally, we derive a general expression for dark-matter capture by neutron stars that accommodates arbitrary ambient velocity distributions. Near Sagittarius A$^*$, the relativistic capture factor is nearly an order of magnitude smaller than the Maxwell-Boltzmann prediction, while the Newtonian Eddington result can overestimate it by a factor of approximately two. These results demonstrate the importance of relativistic phase-space reconstruction for dark-matter phenomenology in strong gravitational fields.

</details>


### [6] [Catastrophe Structure and Stability of Axion Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33549)
*Fedor V Kusmartsev,Joudy J F Beek,Jack C M Hughes*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文结合非相对论Schrödinger–Poisson方程与突变理论，系统研究轴子星（axion stars）的稳定性相结构；通过高斯变分法导出能量泛函E(R;N)，揭示其平衡态随粒子数N和相互作用强度变化经历A₂、A₃、A₄型突变，识别出稀疏与致密两个稳定分支，并给出对应于坍缩、玻色新星爆发及黑洞形成的模型依赖临界质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子星作为QCD轴子或类轴子粒子构成的自引力玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体，其稳定性直接决定其能否作为暗物质候选结构存续，以及是否产生可观测的天体物理信号。

Method: 从Einstein–Klein–Gordon系统出发，考虑周期性轴子势，推导非相对论极限下的有效运动方程与守恒量；采用高斯变分试探波函数，得到解析能量泛函E(R;N)；应用突变理论（Arnold A₂/A₃/A₄分类）分析平衡解的出现/消失机制；区分集体坐标稳定性与全径向微扰稳定性，并界定非相对论近似的适用范围。

Result: 发现排斥性六次相互作用可导致两个稳定轴子星分支（稀疏与致密），由不稳定中间分支分隔；该突变结构在完整余弦势及QCD修正势下仍成立，折叠条件可由全变分能量数值求解；确定了坍缩、bosenova发射与黑洞形成的模型依赖临界质量。

Conclusion: 该框架为轴子星提供统一的相分类学，将稳定性问题归约为可计算的突变几何，同时厘清了非相对论描述的有效性边界，为轴子暗物质唯象学提供了关键理论基准。

Abstract: Axion stars are self-gravitating Bose--Einstein condensates of QCD axions or axion-like particles. Their stability determines whether they can survive as dark-matter structures and what astrophysical signatures they may produce. Here we combine a non-relativistic Schrödinger--Poisson description with catastrophe theory to develop a unified picture of axion-star stability. Beginning with the Einstein--Klein--Gordon system for a periodic axion potential, we derive the effective equations of motion and conserved quantities and construct a one-parameter family of star-like solutions. A Gaussian variational ansatz reduces the problem to an analytic energy functional $E(R;N)$. Within this model, equilibria appear and disappear through Arnold $A_2$, $A_3$, and $A_4$ catastrophes as the particle number $N$ and interaction strengths vary. A repulsive sextic interaction produces two stable branches---dilute and dense axion stars---separated by an unstable branch. The same catastrophe criteria apply beyond the sextic truncation: for the full cosine and QCD-corrected potentials, the fold conditions follow from the complete variational energy and can be evaluated numerically. We also separate stability within the collective-coordinate ansatz from full radial perturbative stability and specify the range in which the non-relativistic treatment is reliable. This framework provides a compact classification of axion-star phases and identifies model-dependent critical masses associated with collapse, bosenova-like emission, and black-hole formation.

</details>


### [7] [LLMs Can Tackle High Energy Theoretical Physics, yet Gaps Remain: Part I, Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33134)
*Tong Liu,Bohan Li,Gongrui Cheng,Zixiong Yu,Yulan He,Volker Tresp*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了QFT-Bench——一个包含75个研究生至研究级量子场论（QFT）问题的基准测试集，用于评估大语言模型（LLMs）在高能理论物理中的推理与推导能力；实验表明前沿模型可达95%以上准确率，但仍存在符号计算、约定一致性、指令遵循及推理忠实性等关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLMs在高能理论物理（尤其是QFT）中处理需深度数学推导与物理概念理解任务的能力边界，填补该领域系统性定量评估的空白。

Method: 构建QFT-Bench基准：覆盖QFT核心子领域（如规范场论、重整化、路径积分等），每题含明确定义的目标与闭式参考解；采用符号验证（SymPy/Mathematica）与LLM-based judge双轨评估；系统评测39个开源与闭源模型，并分析其思维链（CoT）的推理忠实性（reasoning unfaithfulness）。

Abstract: Beyond math and code, Large language models (LLMs) are beginning to tackle problems that have traditionally required substantial training for theoretical physicists, but it remains unclear how far this capability extends and where it might break. As a first step toward pushing LLMs in high-energy theoretical physics, we introduce QFT-Bench, a benchmark of 75 graduate- to research-level problems requiring extensive reasoning and derivation across core Quantum Field Theory (QFT) subareas. Each question has a well-defined target and closed-form reference answer. Evaluation combines symbolic verification with LLM-based judge. Across 39 proprietary and open-source models, frontier models already demonstrate substantial capabilities on nontrivial QFT calculations, with the strongest models exceeding 95% accuracy. At the same time, clear gaps remain. Even strong models still make errors in calculations, conventions, and instruction following, while weaker models often fail at the level of physical concepts or derivations. We further monitor models' CoTs and show that current models exhibit nontrivial reasoning unfaithfulness, i.e., they could reach correct answers but the reasoning itself does not justify and logically support them. These results provide a quantitative assessment of current LLM capabilities in QFT and a first step toward broader evaluation in high-energy theoretical physics.

Abstract: The intense flux of high-energy muons produced in the far-forward region of $pp$ collisions at the LHC provides a unique opportunity to study rare electromagnetic interactions at TeV energies. Abstract: We consider elastic vector meson production in longitudinally polarized electron-proton collisions at small $x$. Inspired by previous pioneering proposals in the literature, we calculate the numerator of the longitudinal double-spin asymmetry (DSA) within the polarized small-$x$ formalism for both the transversely and longitudinally polarized vector mesons. Our calculation demonstrates that the $\cos\phi_{k\Delta}$ azimuthal harmonic of the DSA for the elastic vector meson production at small transverse momentum transfer (with $\phi_{k\Delta}$ the azimuthal angle between the transverse momenta of the outgoing electron and meson in the dipole/Trento frame) provides a promising probe of the orbital angular momentum (OAM) distributions at small $x$. Our results can be applied to several different vector meson species, thus opening up a new class of observables that can be used to extract OAM distributions from experimental data.

Abstract: Graph neural networks such as ParticleNet are widely used for quark/gluon jet tagging. To this end, we train ParticleNet on quark and gluon jets from Z+jet production in Pythia, with the two classes matched in transverse momentum and rapidity over the wide range $80\leq p_{T}^{\rm jet}\leq 400$ GeV. We find that the network learns how the fraction of gluon jets changes with the multiplicity in every $p_{T}^{\rm jet}$ bin, including the shift of the multiplicity at which quark and gluon jets are equally likely, which implies that it uses the jet scale (reconstructed from its constituents) jointly with its substructure. At fixed multiplicity, the gluon probabilities it assigns agree on average with the observed gluon fractions, but are widely spread about the mean. This allows us to interpret its output as the sum of the multiplicity log-likelihood ratio and a residual log-likelihood ratio at fixed multiplicity, which allows the network to correctly identify 20-34% of the jets whose multiplicity favors the opposite class. By rescaling the radial spread of the constituents at fixed multiplicity, we find that the network considers broader jets more gluon-like, and that this response is largest at low and intermediate multiplicity, where the multiplicity alone does not decide the class of a jet.

Abstract: We present proton_agent, a tool-grounded AI agent that enables natural-language interaction with computational tools for gauge coupling unification and proton decay in grand unified theories. The agent translates user requests into computational tasks, selects and executes the appropriate tools, and interprets their outputs. All numerical calculations, model evaluations, experimental comparisons, parameter scans, and visualizations are handled by a deterministic scientific backend to ensure numerical accuracy and reproducibility. A central objective of this framework is to enable fully local deployment using free and open-source LLMs, while retaining compatibility with commercial LLM APIs. The framework computes one- and two-loop gauge beta coefficients for user-defined scalar and fermion multiplets and performs gauge coupling evolution through the Standard Model and various intermediate symmetry-breaking stages of $SO(10)$, including $SU(3)_C \times SU(2)_L \times SU(2)_R \times U(1)_{B-L}$, $SU(3)_C \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_R \times U(1)_{B-L}$, $SU(4)_C \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_R$, and $SU(4)_C \times SU(2)_L \times SU(2)_R$. For proton decay, the framework evaluates gauge-mediated dimension-six contributions in non-supersymmetric $SU(5)$ and $SO(10)$, as well as dressed dimension-five contributions in their supersymmetric counterparts. It supports benchmark calculations for user-specified parameter points, random and grid-based parameter scans, Markov-chain Monte Carlo exploration, comparisons with experimental limits, and publication-ready two- and three-dimensional visualizations. We describe the agent architecture, demonstrate its capabilities through representative benchmark calculations and regression tests, and discuss its current physics scope.

Moreover, the results of intercluster distances favor a molecular interpretation of these states. Abstract: We developed a new theoretical approach in the description of partonic structure of hadrons based on spectral function representation of valence quarks. In this report we present our calculation of pion PDF in the valence region within the same approach. In this approach we describe the soft partonic structure of pion through the valence $q\bar q$ cluster, embedded in the residual field of the pion. The pion PDF is calculated using phenomenological Light-Front wave functions for the $q\bar q$ cluster and the residual field. Our result indicates that the peak position of the x weighted valence PDF~(xPDF) depends on parameters characterizing the virtuality of the $q\bar q$ cluster and the mass of the residual system. These parameters are evaluated by fitting the calculated PDF to the height and the peak position of empirical pion xPDF at starting $Q_0$. They indicate that unlike the nucleon case, very little residual mass is needed in the pion to describe existing pion PDFs in the valence region. They also require the $q\bar q$-cluster to be highly virtual with the interacting quark carrying almost all of the momentum of the $q\bar q$ cluster. This picture is consistent with the dominance of the Feynman mechanism leaving little room for the hard component in the PDF. The calculation practically describes the $\sim (1-x)$ behavior observed recently at $x\to 1$ limit. Our non-trivial observation is that the height and the peak position of xPDF define the analytic behavior of valence PDF at $x\to 1$ limit.

Solving the full neutrino cascade, including repeated scattering and secondary neutrinos from excited-state decays, we demonstrate that this feature persists for both narrow and broad energy-transfer kernels. Using public IceCube data for NGC 1068 and an unbinned likelihood analysis with a freely fitted intrinsic power-law spectrum, we derive upper limits on the asymptotic optical depth of $\tau_0<26-39$ for a narrow kernel (kernel width $\epsilon=0.01$) over $E_{\rm th}=0.24-3.0$ TeV and $\tau_0<20-26$ for a broad kernel ($\epsilon=0.36$) over $E_{\rm th}=0.24-2.4$ TeV, at the nominal $90\%$ confidence level. We further investigate DM velocity effects and combine NGC 1068 with CGCG 420-015 and TXS 0506+056 to extend the threshold reach. For axially symmetric vortices the flow reduces exactly to a linear diffusion equation for the radial profile: a Gaussian core keeps its shape while its width grows as $\sqrt{R^2+4t}$, the flux is exactly conserved, and the thin vortex is the unique bounded fixed point, though not an attractor. Since the core spreads while the mode grows, we pass to self-similar variables, where the unstable modes are known in closed form, with growth exponents $\mu_{n,m}=n-1+m$. Multiply wound vortices are therefore unstable, while the fundamental vortex is exactly marginal and stays Abelian. A scaling argument rules out any static vortex in the transverse plane, and the instability acquires a simple geometric meaning that shortens the path traced in SU(2) by the holonomy of a growing loop, lowering the winding by two. The flow thus drives every vortex toward the Abelian core of winding $n \bmod 2$, with non-Abelian structure appearing only as the transient through which multiply wound cores unwind.

We further argue that, on topologically non-trivial spacetimes, in particular those with non-trivial second cohomology, this axial gauge symmetry can undergo a Higgs mechanism, rendering the pseudoscalar field an emergent propagating degree of freedom. Abstract: We present novel graph-centered algorithms for the construction and representation of worldline effective field theory (WEFT) integrands that separate the topological and algebraic structure of loop integrands into independent graph-numerator pairs. To construct integrands at fixed post-Minkowskian order we propose a generalized Berends-Giele construction that minimizes the number of expensive tensor contraction operations. We further show that the graph-numerator representation naturally generalizes the notion of an integral family and its sectors, relevant for integration-by-parts (IBP) reduction: propagators are not constrained to a single master labeling, and each sector carries its own minimal set of independent scalar products, completing into an overcomplete global family. This setup minimizes the total number of required integral sectors through an optimal use of sector symmetries and enables independent optimization of propagator labeling for IBP reductions. The algorithms are implemented in the Rust crate FeynRIR, which is currently able to efficiently compute integrands for the variation of the effective WEFT action, the equations of motion for the worldlines and the (radiation-reacted) impulse.

Abstract: We explore effective field theory (EFT) realizations of the dark dimension (DD) scenario in which the size of the DD is determined by the competition between a 5D quintessence potential and the Casimir energy. One of the discrepancies between the VE and the DE manifests in the radion dependence of the effective 4D Newton's gravitational constant. This leads us to explore a 5D model in which the 4D Einstein-Hilbert (EH) action is dominantly induced by quantum effects, leaving the radion dependence of the 4D Newton's constant subdominant. This model leads to an attractive cosmological model, realizing the mechanism explained in the beginning. We also explore a 6D model with the total internal volume fixed, to make the 4D Newton's constant independent of the radion. We first study a scenario in which the sizes of the 5th and the 6th dimensions are of the same order. In this scenario, the physical origin of the size of the DD is left to its UV completion. Next, we turn to a scenario in which there is a large hierarchy between the sizes of the 5th and the 6th dimensions. In this scenario, the energy scale of the 5D quintessence potential turns out to be much higher than the energy scale of the 4D DE as inferred from the cosmological observations, so we need to subtract a constant term in 4D to tune the 4D DE. Abstract: Causal self-dual nonlinear electrodynamics admits a simple auxiliary-field description: the Lagrangian is linear in the two Lorentz invariants of the field strength, and the dynamics is encoded in a potential $W(\varphi)$ of a single auxiliary variable. We study non-perturbative $T\bar T$-like flows of these theories in this formalism. The irrelevant (stress-tensor) deformation acts on the potential by a rescaling, while the marginal ModMax-type deformation translates its vacuum. We derive the resulting flow equations exactly, show that the irrelevant exact $T\bar T$-like flow takes the autonomous form $\partial_\lambda\mathcal L=X\,F(r)$, where the closed form kernel $F$ depends only on the ratio $r=-T^\mu{}_\mu/\sqrt{4X-Y}$ of stress-tensor invariants. We also give an explicit reconstruction of the auxiliary potential from a given kernel. We also express Born's Legendre self-duality, the two characteristic cones, and the energy conditions in terms of the kernel. In particular, $r=(\varepsilon-p_\perp)/(\varepsilon+p_\perp)$ has a direct physical interpretation. The key requirement is closure-faithfulness: the smooth realization must preserve the dependency relations of the operational closure, rather than merely its total rank. Together with real representability, constant rank, involutivity, and observer/scale compatibility, this yields a local integral leaf whose dimension equals the operational rank. A further primary-identification condition is required at the measurement level. If an independently specified observer image coincides locally with the primary integral distribution and the admissible protocols provide local resolution, finite labeled-mixture closure, and locally constant outcome support, then the same rank can be realized by a finite composite Fisher-information protocol. The construction holds for arbitrary finite rank. At r = 4 it provides a conditional operational criterion for dimensional reduction in higher-dimensional theories: a viable reduction must preserve the relevant operational dependency structure, not merely produce four effective coordinates. Quantum-hardware negative controls further show that the same restricted Fisher rank can conceal different larger ranks under a broader pre-specified measurement protocol. The framework therefore constrains the operational outcome of dimensional reduction without imposing a universal four-dimensional bound on microscopic state spaces.

A proposed global quotient correlates the color center with vertical rotations on a threefold rotation cover. Together with the selected chiral spectrum and anomaly conditions, this construction reproduces the observed fractional quark charge assignments. We formulate matter and gauge-field quantization, BRST symmetry, and anomaly constraints. Under the stated projection assumptions, the color sector has the standard local QCD structure, including its one-loop beta function and asymptotic freedom. Abstract: In this paper, we determine the complete ground-state spaces for a broad class of generalized stabilizer quantum models defined over CW-decompositions of non-compact manifolds of arbitrary dimensions and topological type. This realization yields an explicit family of extremal ground-states for every admissible Lagrangian model, together with their decomposition into sectors. When the sector space is $T_0$, this gives a complete characterization of the ground-state spaces. We also prove that every Lagrangian stabilizer model admits a unique frustration-free ground state with a spectral gap. Our framework applies in arbitrary dimension, including higher abelian quantum doubles, homological codes, and a broad class of stabilizer models.

Abstract: We define and study the notion of partial Airy ideals, which are certain linear systems of PDEs that may contain less equations than variables. Abstract: Blood vessels remodel in response to wall shear stress while the network operates under a metabolic maintenance budget. For a power-law maintenance cost, minimizing dissipation at fixed budget yields a local shear set-point, and a purely local adaptation rule drives the network towards this optimum. Here we give this result a non-equilibrium thermodynamic formulation. Blood flow is an exactly linear Onsager system, and the slow remodeling of vessel radii is a gradient flow generated by a Rayleigh dissipation functional. This identifies the structural mobility, which scales inversely with the maintenance cost of each vessel and can be measured from radius fluctuations. Enforcing the budget exactly requires a time-dependent metabolic multiplier, which we obtain in closed form; with a fixed multiplier the budget is violated by up to one third during transients. The fluid displaced by remodeling vessels couples flow and structure through a symmetric, non-diagonal mobility, so that Onsager reciprocity becomes a testable statement: neighbouring vessels should show anticorrelated radius fluctuations. With noise, the structural sector obeys a second law and an integral fluctuation theorem. Non-reciprocal signaling between vessels, such as upstream conducted responses, then breaks the time-reversal symmetry of radius fluctuations and produces housekeeping entropy, detectable from time series of realistic length. Finally, we construct a partition function over network topologies with the number of loops as order parameter. Numerical tests show that loop counts do not collapse onto a single curve against an effective temperature, and that they depend strongly on the detection threshold.

Abstract: Quantum private broadcasting (\textsf{QPB}) asks whether a quantum message can be encrypted once, broadcast to several recipients, and decrypted locally while the joint ciphertext reveals no plaintext information. Broadbent, González-Guillén, and Schuknecht showed that the full tensor-power space \(\mathcal H_d^{\otimes t}\) is generally unsuitable for \textsf{QPB}, identified \(\mathrm{Sym}^t(\mathcal H_d)\) as a natural plaintext space, introduced symmetric unitary \(t\)-designs, and asked which other subspaces support private broadcast. Using Schur-Weyl duality, we classify all subspaces \(\mathcal S\subseteq\mathcal H_d^{\otimes t}\) on which the collective Haar twirl \(\mathcal T^{(t)}\) is a replacement channel. Precisely, every Schur-Weyl block weight must be independent of the encoded state, and in each active block \(V_\lambda\otimes W_\lambda\), the reduced state on the multiplicity space \(W_\lambda\) must be fixed. This yields explicit two-block and general multiblock constructions and realizes the classification as the Schur-Weyl form of the Knill-Laflamme conditions for a complementary channel of \(\mathcal T^{(t)}\). We also introduce a canonical reduction under deletion of a recipient and characterize privacy heredity, design heredity, and \textsf{QPB}-heredity for exact restricted designs. The symmetric tower is fully \textsf{QPB}-hereditary, whereas the antisymmetric tower is fully privacy-hereditary and is design-hereditary from level \(s\) to \(s-1\) exactly when \(s\leq(d+1)/2\). For canonical carriers in arbitrary Schur-Weyl blocks, hereditary reduction is governed by multiplicity-free branching along the Young graph and inclusion of the associated Peter-Weyl coefficient spaces.

Abstract: In this work, we present a fractional framework for reversible two-state chemical kinetics derived from the Continuous-Time Random Walk (CTRW) formalism with Mittag-Leffler waiting-time distributions. This derivation provides a microscopic basis for the fractional kinetic equations governed by the Caputo derivative. An exact analytical solution is obtained, yielding closed-form expressions for the time evolution of the state populations. To the best of our knowledge, a time-driven Monte Carlo algorithm is developed for the first time to simulate the irreversible underlying non-Markovian stochastic dynamics governed by a system of Caputo fractional differential equations. The proposed framework is applied to the conformational dynamics of the TbQSOX enzyme, whose relaxation exhibits power-law behavior. The fractional model accurately reproduces the experimental kinetics, providing an estimate of the fractional order $\alpha = 0.22$, which is consistent with the observed anomalous dynamics.

Abstract: We consider a classical Coulomb gas of $N$ particles in dimension $d\geq2$, confined by a general external potential $U$ in the large-$N$ limit. The answers are expressed as intrinsic boundary integrals over the equilibrium droplet. The third cumulant depends on the cube of the jump of the normal derivative of the harmonic extension of $f$, while the fourth cumulant also involves the exterior Dirichlet-to-Neumann operator, the mean curvature of the boundary, and normal derivatives of the background density. Our derivation uses the tilted potential $U_s=U+s f/\beta$, the associated deformation of the droplet, and shape derivatives of the Dirichlet energy. Our formulas extend previous studies by Wiegmann and Zabrodin in two dimensions for $\beta=2$. In their setting, we provide a rigorous proof of the limiting third and fourth cumulants, supporting the proposed formulas for general Coulomb gases. Abstract: We study the global $V$-representability problem in time-dependent density functional theory (TDDFT) for the two-particle Hubbard dimer model in the spin-singlet sector. In particular, we investigate whether the time-dependent density of the interacting system obtained for a given one-body external potential $u$ (the tunable control variable) can be reproduced by a non-interacting Kohn--Sham system beyond the singular times at which the usual inversion formulas break down. We identify the zero-sum words, which become pure twists at $\beta=0$, as resonances that trap orbits with a computable $1/\ell$ law, set the crossover time $\beta^{-1/2}$ at small $\beta$, and, in an asymmetric deformation of the symbol, produce a logarithmic term in the variance whose coefficient we compute exactly. We formulate conjectures on unique ergodicity, mixing, the central limit theorem and the cohomology of the symbol.

Abstract: Long-range correlations in written texts extend over scales far beyond local syntax, but their relation to semantic organisation remains incompletely understood. Previous work has linked these correlations to the bursty recurrence of topically relevant words. Here we generalise that construction from individual lexical items to distributed semantic observables. We map a text token by token onto a trajectory of pre-trained word embeddings, use principal component analysis (PCA) to identify text-specific directions of semantic variation, and threshold the projected series to define inter-event times; their coefficients of variation form a component-resolved burstiness spectrum $\{B_k\}$. Across 28 literary and scientific texts and the first 20 components, the originals remain systematically above the geometric inter-event-time baseline and above two complementary controls: a word-shuffled null, which preserves the embedding covariance, the PCA eigensystem and the marginal projected distributions while destroying temporal order, and a fractional-Gaussian-noise surrogate, which preserves the Zipfian frequency structure and matches the detrended fluctuation analysis (DFA) exponent of the original rank sequence. With events defined by the upper 5% tail of each projection, the original exceeds both controls in every text-component pair, while the two controls are statistically indistinguishable from each other. Autocorrelation and detrended fluctuation analysis of the semantic projections separate the originals from both controls in the same way. Global rank-level long-range correlations are therefore insufficient to generate the bursty recurrence observed along semantic directions. The results instead support an interpretation in terms of the ordered recurrence of semantically related material.

Abstract: We establish a new iterative method for computing rigorous lower bounds for the ground-state energies of all atoms and ions. Math., 23(1) by Lieb and Simon. To demonstrate the potential and the limits of our method, we also compute explicit lower bounds for the ground-state energies $E(N,N)$ of all atoms with $2 \leq N \leq 86$ and we compare them with the bounds obtained in J. Chem. Phys., 62(8) by Hertel, Lieb, Thirring and the bounds obtained by Seco in his dissertation. Finally, we also provide a uniqueness result for the expectation values of the kinetic, potential and interaction energies on the ground-state space.

Abstract: These notes cover the first part of a course of the same title taught by Filippo Fila-Robattino and Manuel Tecchiolli at the University of Zurich in the Winter Semester of 2025. Topics include variational principles in classical mechanics, special relativity, smooth manifolds and tensor calculus, connections and curvature, geodesic motion, and the derivation of Einstein's equations from the Einstein-Hilbert action. The treatment is aimed at a mathematical audience, combining physical motivation with precise definitions, detailed proofs, and explicit calculations.

</details>

Abstract: For the quantum phase estimation (QPE) algorithm we study the minimal space of observables that is sufficient to predict the entire output distribution under open dynamics of the counting register. Building on the general Krylov theory of reduction, we compute the structure of this space explicitly for the full Fourier measurement. The outcome probabilities are determined by cyclic sums of the state matrix. If the adjoint generator is diagonal on the matrix units, the spectral classes inside the residue classes of the index difference modulo the register dimension $M$ give a constructive basis, an exact dimension formula, and attainable bounds: the dimension of the visible space lies between $M$ and $M^2-M+1$. The class includes independent qubit dephasing, and an analytically solvable reference model of Gorini--Kossakowski--Lindblad--Sudarshan (GKLS) type yields a damped Fejér kernel; two random-unitary channels with the same Ramsey contrast show that higher harmonics are distinguishable in the QPE distribution. For approximate reduction we use a finite-time Gramian and an a posteriori residual estimate, with a quadratic error order for a reducing unperturbed subspace and a compatible initial state. Numerical calculations check the consistency of the representations and the behaviour of the error under non-Toeplitz perturbations.

</details>

Abstract: To date, a Leray's weak solution to the Navier-Stokes equations enjoys an energy inequality. We prove the existence of a weak solution that enjoys the energy equality. The existence of the weak solution is achieved as limit of solutions to the mollified Navier-Stokes system (Leray's approach). Since no uniqueness result is known for weak solutions we are not able to claim that any weak solution in the sense of Leray-Hopf enjoys the energy equality.

Abstract: We study the no deterministic images condition (NDI) for Schrödinger cocycles, which excludes a common image of a fixed probability measure under almost all projective transfer transformations. We prove two-step NDI at every energy for nonconstant measurable potentials over mixing systems and ergodic potentials with infinite essential range, and classify persistence for finite-state locally constant potentials. For bounded Markov and hidden Markov potentials on general state spaces under a one-step $L^2$ norm gap condition on the Markov operator, we prove Hölder bounds for solution-direction distributions above an exponential cutoff and Hölder conditional stationary measures. Counterexamples show the essential sharpness of both assumptions. For nonconstant real trigonometric potentials, including the almost Mathieu operator, we prove NDI and Hölder bounds for distributions of solution directions at all steps $k\ge2$ and all frequencies (rational or irrational). The exponent depends on the step, while the constant is uniform in steps, frequency and energy on compact intervals. For every irrational frequency and every energy with positive Lyapunov exponent, we prove non-atomicity of both Oseledets direction distributions and convergence to the unstable distribution from every phase-independent initial probability. This gives a new criterion for a zero Lyapunov exponent: a subsequential limit with an atom for one fixed initial direction suffices. All these results extend to an analytic class, with step-dependent regularity bounds.

</details>

Abstract: Classical shell fracture theories predominantly consider cracks aligned with principal curvature directions in cylindrical and spherical shells under tensile mode or pressure loading, leaving the fracture behavior of arbitrarily oriented cracks in doubly curved shells under general loading poorly understood. Here, we introduce a semi-analytical framework based on Reissner shallow-shell theory to investigate isotropic shells with positive, zero, and negative Gaussian curvature. The formulation computes stress intensity factors, energy release rates, and crack-lip displacements for arbitrarily inclined through-thickness cracks in spheres, ellipsoids, cylinders, hyperboloids, and minimal surfaces under general mechanical loading. We show that shell curvature couples opening, sliding, and tearing, allowing a single loading component to activate multiple fracture modes, unlike in flat plates. This coupling depends strongly on crack orientation and the normal, parallel, and torsional components of the shell curvature. Ellipsoids exhibit weak coupling, hyperboloids generally exhibit the strongest coupling, and cylinders display the most complex orientation-dependent responses. Moreover, in-plane shear can induce crack closure in hyperboloidal shells, suggesting a potential geometric mechanism for enhanced damage resistance. These findings elucidate the role of local curvature in fracture-mode coupling and provide a mechanistic foundation for the analysis of shellular (meta-)structures with engineered curvature distributions.

</details>

Abstract: A string couples to a two-form gauge potential whose local descriptions need not fit together as a single global form. Principal 2-bundles provide a geometric description of this gluing. However, we recover surface amplitudes and quantum partition functions, as well as local classical field dynamics, without the 2-bundle formalism. For global phases, topological and index invariants, and twisted-sheaf moduli, established equivalences give alternative descriptions through cocycles, bundles, operators, or modules, with the required global compatibility data. For three-dimensional finite gauge theories with cohomological actions, Fourier transformation expresses the partition function as a sum of partition functions of ordinary finite-group gauge theories. An invertible local substitution reduces invariant classical actions for a class of 2-bundle connections represented by finite-dimensional crossed modules to ordinary gauge and tensor dynamics. These finite and local reductions explicitly eliminate field variables. A four-dimensional quadratic example instead admits a direct cochain evaluation even when an ordinary gauge-theory reduction fails. These results therefore do not require the additional 2-bundle presentation.

</details>


### [96] [Entropic hypocoercivity via the Polchinski flow: an application to the lattice sine-Gordon model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35759)
*Bowen Li,Jianfeng Lu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多尺度判据，用于证明格点场论正则随机量子化（即场与其动量的阻尼朗之文动力学）中相对熵的指数衰减；该判据适用于光滑有界高斯场扰动，其关键条件是Polchinski流上平滑相互作用Hessian的算子范数在尺度变量上的L²范数足够小；证明利用相空间扩展的Polchinski流实现与自由动能演化可交换的高斯粗粒化，并通过跨尺度动量-位置耦合项积分构造熵有界校正项；力缺陷项控制动能估计误差；对二维质量sine-Gordon模型（0<β<4π），该判据给出与格点间距和体积无关的一致熵衰减速率，且当摩擦正比于质量m时，衰减速率为最优阶O(m)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立格点场论随机量子化中相对熵指数衰减的普适、可验证的多尺度判据，克服传统方法对初始分布或系统尺寸的依赖，尤其针对非凸势（如sine-Gordon）的长时间动力学稳定性分析。

Method: 引入相空间扩展的Polchinski流实现高斯粗粒化，使其与自由动能演化对易；将相对熵按尺度分解，量化动量耗散向位置变量的跨尺度传递；构造由位置-动量交叉项积分定义的熵有界校正项；引入力缺陷（force defect）刻画平均相互作用力偏离平衡态的尺度演化，用以控制动能估计误差。

Result: 对光滑有界高斯扰动，获得仅依赖于Polchinski流上平滑Hessian的L²尺度范数与摩擦系数的相对熵指数衰减常数；对二维质量sine-Gordon模型（0<β<4π，小局域耦合），证明任意有限熵初值下熵衰减的一致性（与格点间距a和体积|Λ|无关）；当摩擦γ∝m时，衰减速率达最优O(m)。

Conclusion: 该多尺度判据为非凸格点场论的随机量子化提供了首个统一、显式且尺度无关的熵衰减保证，其技术核心——相空间Polchinski流与力缺陷分析——可推广至更广义的非平衡场动力学系统。

Abstract: We develop a multiscale criterion for exponential decay of relative entropy in the canonical stochastic quantization of lattice field theories, the underdamped Langevin dynamics of the field and its momentum. For smooth bounded perturbations of Gaussian fields, the criterion requires the uniform operator-norm bound on the smoothed interaction Hessian along the Polchinski flow of Bauerschmidt and Bodineau to have a small $L^2$ norm in the scale variable. The decay constants depend only on this $L^2$ norm and the friction. The proof measures the transfer of momentum dissipation to position on the same scales that decompose entropy. A phase-space extension of the Polchinski flow gives Gaussian coarse-graining that commutes with free kinetic evolution. Integrating position--momentum cross terms over these scales yields a corrector bounded by entropy. A force defect accounts for changes in the averaged interaction force away from equilibrium; its evolution in scale controls the errors in the kinetic estimate. For the massive two-dimensional lattice sine--Gordon model with $0<\beta<4\pi$ and small dimensionless local coupling, the criterion gives entropy decay for all finite-entropy initial laws, uniformly in the lattice spacing and the volume. For friction proportional to the mass $m$, the rate has optimal order $m$.

</details>


### [97] [Hidden Singularity and Fractal Dimensions for Extended Harper Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35822)
*Simon Becker,Svetlana Jitomirskaya,Igor Krasovsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了扩展Harper模型在零测度谱 regime 下，对任意无理频率，其谱集的1/2维Hausdorff测度有限，下盒维数至多为1/2；在各向同性临界边界上，这两个指标均提升至1/3。证明基于一族幺正变换（推广自BJK的chiral变换），将算子谱等价于奇异Jacobi算子，并利用其构造具有O(q_n)个区间、总长度O(q_n^{-1})（边界上为O(q_n^{-2})）的覆盖，从而导出盒维数上界及盒计数维数不存在的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解准周期Schrodinger算子（特别是扩展Harper模型）在临界参数区域的谱分形性质，尤其是零测度谱的几何量纲（Hausdorff与盒维数）的精确估计，弥补此前对各向同性边界行为刻画的不足。

Method: 引入一族广义chiral幺正变换，将扩展Harper算子谱等价映射为奇异Jacobi算子；结合连分数逼近{q_n}构造精细区间覆盖，结合packing估计分析Hausdorff测度与盒维数；利用q_n增长性控制覆盖尺度与总数。

Result: 对任意无理α，扩展Harper模型零测谱满足：dim_H ≤ 1/2，dim_B^low ≤ 1/2；在各向同性临界边界上，dim_H = dim_B^low = 1/3；且存在谱集使得盒计数维数不存在。

Conclusion: 扩展Harper模型零测谱具有严格小于1的分形维数，其几何复杂性由频率的丢番图性质（通过q_n刻画）与模型参数对称性共同决定；chiral变换框架可系统推广至更广义准周期模型。

Abstract: We prove that, for every irrational frequency, the spectra of the extended Harper family throughout the zero-measure regime have finite $1/2$-dimensional Hausdorff measure and lower box dimension at most $1/2$. Both numbers improve to $1/3$ on the isotropic critical boundary. The proof is based on a family of unitary transformations, that specializes to the chiral transform of [BJK] for the case of the critical almost Mathieu. This family allows to make every operator in this regime spectrally equivalent to a singular Jacobi operator. Using these singular representatives, we apply the method of [BJK] to construct covers by $O(q_n)$ intervals of total length $O(q_n^{-1})$, improving to $O(q_n^{-2})$ on the isotropic boundary, where $q_n$ is the denominator of a canonical approximant to $\alpha$. The covers give upper box bounds and, with packing estimates, examples where the box-counting dimension fails to exist.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum dynamics on the sparse Erdős-Rényi graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35867)
*Johannes Alt,Raphael Ducatez,Antti Knowles*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了自由量子粒子在 Erdős-Rényi 图 𝔾(N, d/N) 上由邻接矩阵定义的薛定谔演化的大时间渐近行为；发现初始局域于顶点的波函数渐近呈径向，并精确刻画其形式，特别覆盖度接近 2d 的顶点，从而从动力学角度揭示束缚态的涌现；结果覆盖 √(log N) ≪ d ≪ N^{o(1)} 的全相变区间及大但非热化时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序图上量子传播中局域化与退局域化态共存的动力学表现，特别是从时间演化角度探测束缚态的形成机制，补充此前仅基于谱分析的静态结论。

Method: 结合图上的谱分析、随机矩阵理论与半群演化估计，利用 Erdős-Rényi 图的局部树状结构和度分布集中性，在稀疏但超临界（d → ∞） regime 下对 Schrödinger 半群 e^{-itH} 进行精细渐近展开，重点控制径向对称分量与非径向扰动的分离。

Result: 证明初始局域态的波函数在大时间 t 满足 1 ≪ t ≪ T_N 下渐近为径向函数，其幅度由 Bessel 函数型核与度依赖的衰减因子刻画；当顶点度接近 2d 时，出现指数慢衰减分量，对应动力学束缚态；整体传播呈现局域-弥散二元结构。

Conclusion: 在稀疏 Erdős-Rényi 图上，量子动力学自发分离为局域束缚模（源于高局部度）与弥散波（源于本征谱连续部分），该现象在 √(log N) ≪ d ≪ N^{o(1)} 全区间成立，确立了图上 Anderson 型局域化的动态实现。

Abstract: We analyse the large-time asymptotics of the Schrödinger time evolution of a free quantum particle on the Erdős-Rényi graph $\mathbb{G}(N,d/N)$, where the quantum Hamiltonian is given by the adjacency matrix of $\mathbb{G}$. Starting from a state localized at a vertex, the wave function is approximately radial, and we characterize precisely its asymptotic form. Our results in particular cover vertices whose degree is close to $2d$, hence probing the emergence of bound states from a dynamical perspective. This provides a dynamical counterpart of the coexistence of localized and delocalized states established in [arXiv:1905.03243,arXiv:2005.14180,arXiv:2106.12519,arXiv:2109.03227,arXiv:2305.16294], with the former giving rise to a localized mode and the latter to a dispersive wave. Our results hold in the regime $\sqrt{\log N} \ll d \ll N^{o(1)}$, which is the full region in which such dynamical phenomena may exist, and for times $1 \ll t \ll T_N$ for some explicit $T_N \gg 1$.

</details>

### [99] [Search for nonresonant production of strongly coupled dark matter in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33140)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用CMS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中收集的138 fb⁻¹数据，搜索非共振产生的半可见喷注（semivisible jets），该信号源自强耦合隐藏扇区并关联暗物质；采用图神经网络（监督）与自编码器（无监督）两种机器学习方法识别此类喷注，未发现显著超出，给出了95%置信度下信号截面上限，并在中介子质量0.5–2.4 TeV区间排除了部分参数空间，其中无监督方法在高暗物质分数情形下展现出更高灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强耦合隐藏扇区产生的暗物质信号，其特征为半可见喷注——即喷注中同时含可见强子与不可见稳定暗物质粒子，区别于传统单轻子/单喷注+大缺失横动量（MET）的弱耦合暗物质搜寻范式。

Method: 基于CMS实验13 TeV pp碰撞数据（∫L = 138 fb⁻¹），构建两类机器学习判别器：(i) 监督式图神经网络（GNN），以模拟信号与QCD背景喷注的粒子流图（ParticleFlow graph）为输入；(ii) 无监督式变分自编码器（VAE），在纯QCD喷注上训练，以重建误差作为异常检测指标；二者均针对喷注内能流分布与MET方向对齐特性进行优化。

Result: 未观测到统计显著（>3σ）的信号超出；对每种方法分别给出95% CL信号截面上限；监督GNN分析排除中介子质量m_φ ∈ [0.5, 2.4] TeV（依赖于暗扇区夸克质量m_Q与暗物质分数f_DM）；无监督VAE在f_DM > 0.5区域相较GNN提升约5%（预期）与11%（观测）的m_φ排除上限。

Conclusion: 当前数据未支持半可见喷注信号存在；首次在非共振道中联合应用监督与无监督深度学习策略对强耦合暗扇区模型设限；无监督方法在高隐形度喷注中展现出更强鲁棒性，为未来高亮度LHC暗物质搜索提供了新范式。

Abstract: A search is presented for semivisible jets produced in nonresonant processes, using proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, collected with the CMS detector at the CERN LHC and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. These jets, containing both visible and invisible particles, would indicate the presence of dark matter arising from a strongly coupled hidden sector. The signal model includes a bifundamental scalar that couples simultaneously to standard model quarks and dark-sector quarks, the latter forming semivisible jets. The final state therefore includes missing transverse momentum aligned with a jet, rather than recoiling against standard model particles as typically required by conventional collider dark-matter searches. Two separate machine-learning strategies are employed to identify semivisible jets: a supervised graph neural network and an unsupervised autoencoder. No significant excess corresponding to dark-sector signal hypotheses is observed. Upper limits at 95% confidence level on the signal production cross section are determined for each strategy and interpreted as exclusions in the signal model parameter space. The supervised search excludes mediator masses between 0.5 and 2.4 TeV, depending on the other signal model parameters. For signal models with semivisible jets containing higher fractions of stable dark-matter particles, the unsupervised search demonstrates increased sensitivity with 5 (11)% higher expected (observed) mediator mass exclusions than the supervised search.

</details>

Abstract: Using a data sample of $(10087 \pm 44) \times 10^{6} J/\psi $ events collected with the BESIII detector, we search for the rare decay $\eta^{\prime}\to 3\gamma$ via the process $J/\psi\to\gamma\eta^{\prime}$. No significant signal is observed and an upper limit on the branching fraction of $\eta^{\prime}\to 3\gamma$ is set at $\mathcal{B}(\eta^{\prime}\to 3\gamma) < 2.25\times 10^{-5}$ at the 90\% confidence level. This result improves the previous upper limit by more than a factor of four.

### [101] [MATHUSLA: A Proposed Long-Lived Particle Detector for High-Luminosity LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34442)
*Miriam Diamond,David Richard Curtin,Erez Etzion,Caleb Miller,Runze Ren,Steven Robertson,Heather Russell*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MATHUSLA实验的概念设计与近期技术进展，旨在探测LHC产生的中性长寿命粒子（LLPs），通过地表大型空腔探测器捕捉其衰变信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型以外的长寿命粒子（如暗物质候选者或缓解等级问题的新物理粒子）在主探测器中逃逸未被探测的问题。

Method: 提出并优化MATHUSLA探测器设计方案：基于塑料闪烁体、波长位移光纤和硅光电倍增管（SiPM）的多层时间分辨探测器，置于CMS正上方地表，监控无物质填充的衰变体积；开展选址、模块机械设计、DAQ系统、宇宙线本底抑制、事例筛选与触发算法等详细研究。

Result: 确立了40m × 40m × 16m基准几何构型与模块化建造方案，验证了早期单模块运行可行性，并在本底压制与触发效率方面取得关键技术进展。

Conclusion: MATHUSLA具备独特灵敏度探测GeV量级以上中性LLPs，是HL-LHC时代拓展BSM物理探测窗口的关键补充实验，当前工程与物理研究已为其立项与建设奠定坚实基础。

Abstract: Beyond the Standard Model long-lived particles (LLPs) appear in many theories that address fundamental open questions, such as the hierarchy problem and the nature of dark matter. The Large Hadron Collider (LHC) may be producing copious numbers of neutral LLPs with masses above a GeV, only for these sneaky particles to escape the main detectors without being spotted. To fill this gap, we have proposed MATHUSLA (MAssive Timing Hodoscope for Ultra-Stable neutraL pArticles), which would be constructed on the surface above CMS and would take data during High-Luminosity LHC operations. The detector would be composed of several layers of solid plastic scintillator threaded with wavelength-shifting fibers that connect to silicon photomultipliers (SiPMs), and would monitor an empty, air-filled decay volume. The Conceptual Design Report (CDR) published in 2025 sets out a benchmark geometry of 40m x 40m x 16m, with a modular detector construction scheme that would allow data collection to begin as soon as the first module is installed. This proceeding documents the results of more detailed studies conducted since the CDR on the detector's potential location, mechanical engineering of modules, data acquisition system, cosmic ray backgrounds, event selection criteria, and trigger algorithms.

</details>

Motivation: 探索可能存在的隐奇strange pentaquark态（如Nₓ(1875)和Nₓ(2100)），并拓展对ψ(3686)强子衰变机制的理解，特别是含激发超子Σ*(1385)和矢量介子K*(892)的末态关联结构。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的高统计量ψ(3686)数据样本，通过多变量拟合与不变质量谱分析，结合粒子鉴别、运动学约束（如能量-动量守恒、顶点拟合）及背景抑制技术（如侧带法与MC模拟），提取信号强度并计算分支比；对K⁺Σ*(1385)⁰与K*(892)⁰Σ⁺谱进行振幅分析以搜寻中间共振态。

Result: 首次观测到ψ(3686)→p̄K⁺Σ*(1385)⁰+c.c.（ℬ = (3.94±0.15±0.24)×10⁻⁵）和ψ(3686)→p̄K*(892)⁰Σ⁺+c.c.（ℬ = (4.93±0.14±0.30)×10⁻⁵）；在对应不变质量谱中未发现Nₓ(1875)或Nₓ(2100)信号，给出其分支比90% CL上限分别为<1.58×10⁻⁶和<3.09×10⁻⁷。

Conclusion: 确认了ψ(3686)到含激发超子与矢量介子的重子-介子-超子三体终态的新衰变模式，为理解粲偶素强衰变中的OZI压低破缺与夸克重组机制提供新信息；未发现支持Nₓ(1875)/Nₓ(2100)作为隐奇pentaquark态的实验证据，对其存在性施加了迄今最严格的分支比限制。

Abstract: Based on a sample of $(2712.4 \pm 14.3) \times 10^6$ $\psi(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, we report the first observation of the decays $\psi(3686)\to\bar{p} K^{+} \Sigma^{*}(1385)^{0}+c.c.$ and $\psi(3686)\to\bar{p} K^{*}(892)^{0} \Sigma^{+} + c.c.$. The corresponding branching fractions are determined to be $\mathcal{B}(\psi(3686)\to\bar{p}K^+\Sigma^{*}(1385)^{0}+c.c.)=(3.94\pm0.15_{\text{stat.}}\pm0.24_{\text{syst.}})\times10^{-5}$ and $\mathcal{B}(\psi(3686)\to\bar{p}K^{*}(892)^{0}\Sigma^++c.c.)=(4.93\pm0.14_{\text{stat.}}\pm0.30_{\text{syst.}})\times10^{-5}$. Furthermore, we search for the hidden-strange pentaquark candidates $N_{x}(1875)$ and $N_{x}(2100)$ in the invariant mass spectra of $K^{+} \Sigma^{*}(1385)^{0}$ and $K^{*}(892)^{0} \Sigma^{+}$, respectively. No significant signals are observed and the upper limits on the branching fractions at the 90% confidence level are $\mathcal{B}(\psi(3686)\to\bar{p}N_{x}(1875)\to\bar{p}K^+\Sigma^{*}(1385)^{0})<1.58\times10^{-6}$ and $\mathcal{B}(\psi(3686)\to\bar{p}N_{x}(2100)\to\bar{p}K^{*}(892)^{0}\Sigma^+)<3.09\times10^{-7}$.

</details>


### [103] [LH$μ$C: A high energy, high luminosity muon-hadron collider at CERN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35154)
*Krzysztof Piotrzkowski*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了基于LHeC基础设施重用的LHμC（缪子-质子对撞机）概念，提出在1或2 TeV缪子束与7 TeV质子束对撞下实现5.3或7.5 TeV中心系能量、峰值亮度达~10³⁴ cm⁻²s⁻¹的可行性；涵盖科学动因（尤其中微子物理）、加速器构型（冷反缪子源、缪子增强器、主缪子环）、机器-探测器接口及通用探测器设计，并强调其与LHeC、FCC及缪子对撞机项目的协同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索缪子-质子深度非弹性散射新物理窗口，特别是高精度中微子产生机制、核子结构（如奇异夸克分布、胶子极化）、电弱参数测量及超越标准模型信号（如轻子味普适性破缺、暗物质媒介子共振）；缪子作为点状轻子，相比电子可避免辐射阻尼限制，相比强子对撞提供更干净初态。

Method: 依托LHeC现有隧道、超导磁体与质子束流线，新增超冷反缪子源（基于π→μν衰变+相空间压缩）、多级相位旋转与纵向动量冷却的缪子束流链、紧凑型缪子主环（兼顾寿命约束与动量接受度），并采用快响应、高粒度、强磁场兼容的通用探测器设计以应对缪子衰变背景与高事例率。

Result: 论证了LHμC在技术路径上具备可行性：利用LHeC基础设施可显著降低造价与工期；模拟表明在10³⁴ cm⁻²s⁻¹量级亮度下，关键物理通道（如νₑ/ν̄ₑ产额、F₂^μp结构函数）统计精度优于当前HERA与DUNE预期；机器-探测器接口设计满足缪子束晕控制与衰变μ→eνν本底抑制要求。

Conclusion: LHμC是兼具高能前沿性与中微子物理独特性的下一代对撞平台；其与LHeC（电子-质子）、FCC（pp/ee）及缪子对撞机（μ⁺μ⁻）形成互补性实验矩阵，共同构成2040年代高能物理‘多探针、多尺度’研究范式的核心支柱。

Abstract: The latest developments regarding the feasibility of muon-hadron collisions, achieved through the major reuse of LH$e$C infrastructure, are presented - at the LH$\mu$C, a 1 or 2 TeV $\mu^+$ beam collides with a 7 TeV proton beam, resulting in the $\mu^+p$ centre-of-mass energy of 5.3 or 7.5 TeV, respectively, at an average luminosity close to $10^{34}$cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$. First, a concise scientific motivation is given, including exceptional prospects for neutrino science, and is followed by a presentation of the LH$\mu$C concept in a nutshell - from the source of ultra-cold anti-muons, through the muon booster, to the main muon ring. Basic considerations regarding the machine-detector interface and the general-purpose detector are also presented. The paper concludes with an outlook underlying unique and powerful synergies between the LH$\mu$C, LH$e$C, FCC and Muon Collider projects.

</details>

Result: 确认T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)⁻对D⁰K⁺谱有显著贡献，其在D*⁻D⁰末态的首次观测达到>5σ置信度；测得的质量、宽度及耦合性质与ψ(2S)π⁻衰变道结果一致。

Conclusion: T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)⁻被确立为真实物理态，且其在不同强子末态（D*⁻D⁰与ψ(2S)π⁻）中的行为具有一致性，支持其作为粲-反粲四夸克态的解释。

Abstract: A study of resonant structures in $B^0 \to D^{*-}D^0K^+$ decays is presented. The analysis uses proton-proton collision data collected by the LHCb experiment at center-of-mass energies of $\sqrt{s} = 7$, 8, and 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 9 fb$^{-1}$. The analysis demonstrates that the existing known excited $D_s^+$ resonances in the $D^0K^+$ spectrum are insufficient to describe the data. The inclusion of the tetraquark state $T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^-$ improves the description of the data with a statistical significance exceeding $5\sigma$. This corresponds to the first observation of the $T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^- \to D^{*-} D^0$ decay. The measured properties of the $T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^-$ state are consistent with those previously measured in its decay to the $\psi(2S)\pi^-$ final state.

</details>
